# Graph Report - Visao_V2  (2026-11-12)

## Corpus Check
- 2867 files · ~2,121,272 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 21663 nodes · 37626 edges · 1854 communities (1444 shown, 410 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 265 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `6dd2f407`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
